const express = require('express');
const sqlite3 = require('sqlite3').verbose();
const path = require('path');

const app = express();
const PORT = 3000;

// رفع حد حجم البيانات المسموح به لاستيعاب الإكسل والأجهزة الكثيرة
app.use(express.json({ limit: '100mb' }));
app.use(express.urlencoded({ limit: '100mb', extended: true }));
app.use(express.static(__dirname));

const dbFile = path.join(__dirname, 'database.sqlite');
const db = new sqlite3.Database(dbFile, (err) => {
    if (err) {
        console.error("خطأ في الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات:", err.message);
    } else {
        console.log("تم الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات بنجاح وبأداء عالي!");
    }
});

// تفعيل وضع السرعة القصوى في SQLite
db.run("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
db.run("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;");

// إنشاء جداول مستقلة لكل قسم
db.serialize(() => {
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS system_store (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT)`);
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, data TEXT)`);
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS movements (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, data TEXT)`);
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS maintenance (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, data TEXT)`);
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, data TEXT)`);
});

// مسار لجلب البيانات مهيكلة وسريعة جداً
app.get('/api/system-data', (req, res) => {
    const result = {};
    
    db.all(`SELECT * FROM system_store`, [], (err, storeRows) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        storeRows.forEach(row => {
            try { result[row.key] = JSON.parse(row.value); } catch(e) {}
        });

        db.all(`SELECT data FROM devices`, [], (err, devRows) => {
            if (!err && devRows) {
                result.devices = devRows.map(r => JSON.parse(r.data));
            }
            
            db.all(`SELECT data FROM movements`, [], (err, movRows) => {
                if (!err && movRows) {
                    result.movements = movRows.map(r => JSON.parse(r.data));
                }

                db.all(`SELECT data FROM maintenance`, [], (err, maintRows) => {
                    if (!err && maintRows) {
                        result.maintenance = maintRows.map(r => JSON.parse(r.data));
                    }

                    db.all(`SELECT data FROM users`, [], (err, userRows) => {
                        if (!err && userRows && userRows.length > 0) {
                            result.users = userRows.map(r => JSON.parse(r.data));
                        }
                        res.json(result);
                    });
                });
            });
        });
    });
});

// مسار لحفظ وتحديث البيانات بسرعة فائقة ودون أخطاء تكرار المعرفات
app.post('/api/system-data', (req, res) => {
    const payload = req.body;

    db.serialize(() => {
        db.run("BEGIN TRANSACTION");

        // حفظ الإعدادات العامة والأقسام الأخرى
        const storeKeys = ['towers', 'customWarehouses', 'targetStaffAssignments', 'categoryModelsMap', 'itemTypesCategoriesMap', 'categoryAssignedFieldsMap', 'customSystemFields', 'brandingSettings', 'uiCustomization', 'tabVisibility'];
        storeKeys.forEach(key => {
            if (payload[key] !== undefined) {
                db.run(`INSERT OR REPLACE INTO system_store (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)`, [key, JSON.stringify(payload[key])]);
            }
        });

        // تحديث الأجهزة في جدول منفصل مع معالجة فريدة لـ id لمنع الانهيار
        if (Array.isArray(payload.devices)) {
            db.run(`DELETE FROM devices`);
            const stmtDev = db.prepare(`INSERT OR REPLACE INTO devices (id, data) VALUES (?, ?)`);
            payload.devices.forEach((d, index) => {
                const uniqueId = Number(d.id) || (Date.now() + index + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000));
                stmtDev.run(uniqueId, JSON.stringify(d));
            });
            stmtDev.finalize();
        }

        // تحديث الحركات
        if (Array.isArray(payload.movements)) {
            db.run(`DELETE FROM movements`);
            const stmtMov = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO movements (data) VALUES (?)`);
            payload.movements.forEach(m => {
                stmtMov.run(JSON.stringify(m));
            });
            stmtMov.finalize();
        }

        // تحديث الصيانة
        if (Array.isArray(payload.maintenance)) {
            db.run(`DELETE FROM maintenance`);
            const stmtMaint = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO maintenance (data) VALUES (?)`);
            payload.maintenance.forEach(m => {
                stmtMaint.run(JSON.stringify(m));
            });
            stmtMaint.finalize();
        }

        // تحديث المستخدمين
        if (Array.isArray(payload.users)) {
            db.run(`DELETE FROM users`);
            const stmtUser = db.prepare(`INSERT OR REPLACE INTO users (id, data) VALUES (?, ?)`);
            payload.users.forEach((u, index) => {
                const uniqueUserId = Number(u.id) || (Date.now() + index);
                stmtUser.run(uniqueUserId, JSON.stringify(u));
            });
            stmtUser.finalize();
        }

        db.run("COMMIT", (err) => {
            if (err) {
                console.error("خطأ في تنفيذ المعاملة:", err.message);
                res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
            } else {
                res.json({ success: true });
            }
        });
    });
});

app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`الخادم السريع جداً يعمل على الرابط: http://localhost:${PORT}`);
});
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>النظام الشامل لإدارة أصول ومخازن شبكة يمن نت v4.9 (سريع جداً)</title>
    <!-- Bootstrap 5 RTL CSS -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css">
    <!-- FontAwesome Icons -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.4.0/css/all.min.css">
    <!-- Google Fonts (Cairo) -->
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <!-- Chart.js for Dashboards -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
    
    <!-- SheetJS (XLSX) library for importing Excel/CSV files -->
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xlsx/0.18.5/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>

    <style>
        :root {
            --bs-body-font-size: 1rem;
            --bs-body-color: #212529;
            --theme-primary: #0d6efd;
            --theme-bg: #f1f5f9;
        }
        body { font-family: 'Cairo', sans-serif; background-color: var(--theme-bg); font-size: var(--bs-body-font-size); color: var(--bs-body-color); }
        
        #loginScreen {
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100vw;
            height: 100vh;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0f172a 0%, #1e293b 100%);
            z-index: 100000;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
        }
        .login-card {
            background: #f8f9fa;
            border-radius: 16px;
            box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 420px;
            padding: 40px;
        }

        .sidebar {
            min-height: 100vh;
            background: #0f172a;
            color: #fff;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
            width: 16.666667%;
        }
        .sidebar .nav-link {
            color: #94a3b8;
            padding: 12px 15px;
            font-weight: 600;
            transition: 0.3s;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            white-space: nowrap;
            overflow: hidden;
            cursor: pointer;
        }
        .sidebar .nav-link i {
            font-size: 1.1rem;
            min-width: 25px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .sidebar .nav-link:hover, .sidebar .nav-link.active {
            color: #fff;
            background: #334155;
            border-radius: 8px;
        }
        
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar {
            width: 75px !important;
            padding-left: 5px !important;
            padding-right: 5px !important;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar .nav-link span,
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar h4 span,
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar .footer {
            display: none !important;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar h4 {
            font-size: 0;
            text-align: center;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar h4 i {
            margin: 0 !important;
            font-size: 1.4rem;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar .nav-link {
            justify-content: center;
            padding: 12px 0;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .sidebar .nav-link i {
            margin-left: 0 !important;
        }
        body.sidebar-minimized .main-content-area {
            width: calc(100% - 75px) !important;
        }

        .main-content-area {
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
        }

        .stat-card { border-radius: 12px; border: none; transition: transform 0.2s; box-shadow: 0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05); }
        .stat-card:hover { transform: translateY(-4px); }
        .badge-status { font-size: 0.85rem; padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 20px; }
        
        /* تخصيص الترتيب ليقتصر التحديد والتحويم على سهم الفرز فقط */
        th.sortable {
            cursor: pointer;
            user-select: none;
        }
        th.sortable .fa-sort {
            opacity: 0.4;
            margin-right: 5px;
            padding: 3px 6px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        th.sortable:hover .fa-sort {
            opacity: 1;
            background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
        }

        #floatingNotifications {
            position: fixed;
            bottom: 20px;
            left: 20px;
            z-index: 9999;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 10px;
            max-width: 350px;
        }
        .toast-notification {
            background: #ffffff;
            border-right: 5px solid var(--theme-primary);
            box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
            padding: 12px 15px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            animation: slideInLeft 0.3s ease-out forwards;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
        }
        @keyframes slideInLeft {
            from { transform: translateX(-100%); opacity: 0; }
            to { transform: translateX(0); opacity: 1; }
        }
        @media print {
            .sidebar, .btn-group, .modal, .no-print, .input-group, .top-header-bar { display: none !important; }
            .col-md-9, .col-lg-10 { width: 100% !important; }
            body { background: #fff !important; }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

<!-- شاشة تسجيل الدخول -->
<div id="loginScreen">
    <div class="login-card">
        <div class="text-center mb-4">
            <div class="bg-primary text-white rounded-circle d-inline-flex align-items-center justify-content-center mb-3 shadow" style="width: 70px; height: 70px; font-size: 2rem;">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-server"></i>
            </div>
            <h4 class="fw-bold text-dark">تسجيل الدخول للنظام</h4>
            <p class="text-muted small">النظام الشامل لإدارة أصول شبكة يمن نت </p>
        </div>
        
        <div id="loginAlert"></div>

        <form id="loginForm" onsubmit="handleLogin(event)">
            <div class="mb-3">
                <label class="form-label fw-bold">اسم المستخدم</label>
                <div class="input-group">
                    <span class="input-group-text bg-light"><i class="fa-solid fa-user text-muted"></i></span>
                    <input type="text" id="loginUsername" class="form-control" required placeholder="أدخل اسم المستخدم" autocomplete="username">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="mb-4">
                <label class="form-label fw-bold">كلمة المرور</label>
                <div class="input-group">
                    <span class="input-group-text bg-light"><i class="fa-solid fa-lock text-muted"></i></span>
                    <input type="password" id="loginPassword" class="form-control" required autocomplete="current-password" placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور">
                </div>
            </div>
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary w-100 py-2 fw-bold shadow-sm">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-right-to-bracket me-2"></i> دخول للنظام
            </button>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

<div id="floatingNotifications"></div>

<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row flex-nowrap">
        <!-- Sidebar Navigation -->
        <div class="col-auto sidebar p-3 d-flex flex-column no-print" id="sidebarNav">
            <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary pb-3 mb-2">

                    <i class="fa-solid fa-server me-2"></i><span>نظام شبكة يمن نت</span>
